# HANDOFF_REPORT.md — CFHM F1-v2

> **Originated by:** Ox-alpha. **Relayed by:** User. **Implemented and executed by:** Manus. **Audited by:** Independent reviewer, pending v2 audit.

## S1 — Provenance

The F1-v1 baseline remains tagged at `F1-v1-baseline` → `594c6793a4a71ce55f164865ef9525b54d9678eb` and was not modified. F1-v2 was executed in the isolated `f1_v2/` root. The repository HEAD at report construction is `afbf4b1fa1e348107d37a17a228c2577d7383ed9`. The R6/R7 registration commit is `3134c94c9a8a3733a0a69092fddd8d6afe2eeeaa`; the R6 minimal gamma commit is `2ab23143a28249de2c110199f69779d2c4f89660`; the v2 forecast-shape fix and calibration/output implementation commit is `41f258e1e197e273bc3c4e6c7937068b67e91fea`.

Commands executed for v2 were:

```text
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --root /home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/f1_v2 --seeds 1000:1000
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --root /home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/f1_v2 --seeds 1001:1024 --append
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --root /home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/f1_v2 --seeds 1025:1049 --append
python3 compute_verdict.py --root /home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/f1_v2
python3 score_metrics.py f1_v2/predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv --output-json f1_v2/metrics/metrics_recomputed.json
```

## S6 — Independent recomputation

The shared scorer processed the complete predictions CSV: 80000 data rows, expected 80000. The source metrics table has 400 rows and the independent scorer output has 400 rows. Maximum absolute delta across Precision@25, Precision@10, and AUROC after key-based reconciliation is `5.55111512313e-17`. Row-count reconciliation and metric equality: `True`.

## S9 — `DEVIATIONS.md` verbatim

```markdown
# Deviations and Execution Errors

## DEVIATION-001 — Repository commit identity unavailable

**Stage:** Repository initialization, before experiment implementation.

**Exact error:**

```text
Author identity unknown
*** Please tell me who you are.

Run

git config --global user.email "you@example.com"
git config --global user.name "Your Name"

to set your account's default identity.
Omit --global to set the identity only in this repository.
fatal: unable to auto-detect email address (got 'ubuntu@db71298c03d4.(none)')
```

**Classification:** Implementation/environment bookkeeping failure. This is not a scientific-design deviation and did not alter the authored mechanism, data generator, baselines, metrics, or verdict criteria.

**Resolution status:** Unresolved at time of recording. A repository-local, non-personal identity may be configured solely to create the required provenance commit; no global identity or authored specification will be changed.

**Scientific impact:** None observed; no experiment code has been executed.

## Pending specification blockers

To be completed after the executable requirements audit. No implementation assumptions have been introduced.

## DEVIATION-002 — SPEC-001-R1 still contains material execution blockers

**Stage:** Post-resolution specification audit, before experiment implementation.

The following issues were discovered while checking SPEC-001-R1 against the binding executor rules. No scientific implementation has been started.

### R1-BLOCK-001 — Maintainer firing probability contradicts its effective probability

R-006 states: `Firing: Bernoulli(1/26) per week`, then states for `k` firing maintainers the child has an event with `effective p = 1 − 0.5^k`. Those are incompatible: if each maintainer fires with probability `1/26`, the probability that at least one of `k` maintainers fires is `1 − (25/26)^k`, not `1 − 0.5^k`. R-006 also says forced child events override the child's Bernoulli draw and set event = 1. The author must specify which probability governs maintainer firing or the intended meaning of the `0.5` term.

**Classification:** Scientific data-generator ambiguity. No choice made.

### R1-BLOCK-002 — CFHM learned recursion remains referenced but not explicitly written

R-001 says the complete learned forward pass is inlined in R-002/R-003/R-008/R-013, but those sections specify ground-truth tap states, parameter distributions/reparameterizations, and the forecast freeze only. They do not explicitly state the learned hazard recursion, the exact use of model-visible typed edges, or the learned state update and indexing. The original SPEC-001 still refers to the absent “§4 recursion from the design.” The execution layer cannot certify that an inferred analog is exactly authored.

**Classification:** Scientific model-definition omission. No choice made.

### R1-BLOCK-003 — A2 power-law amplitude mapping is unspecified

R-002 defines A2 ground-truth excitation as a single-channel power-law state, while R-003 still defines typed, three-tap `b*_{τ,m}` values. It is not stated whether A2 uses a type-specific scalar, the sum of the three tap amplitudes, one selected tap, or another mapping to multiply the single-channel state. This changes the ground-truth mechanism.

**Classification:** Scientific data-generator ambiguity. No choice made.

### R1-BLOCK-004 — Bootstrap duplicate-node construction and zero-copy predictions are undefined

R-014 requires resampling 200 nodes with replacement, rebuilding the series for the sampled multiset, and assigning each original node the mean over its copies. It does not define the graph/features/state construction for duplicate nodes, nor the prediction for an original node receiving zero copies in a bootstrap replicate. This affects the Kendall τ stability gate.

**Classification:** Measurement/stability-procedure ambiguity. No choice made.

### R1-BLOCK-005 — RNG key type for PyTorch is undefined

R-016 defines `SeedSequence(seed).spawn(6)` keys consumed by `numpy.default_rng`, then requires `torch.manual_seed(world_key + 4000)`. A spawned NumPy `SeedSequence` is not directly addable to an integer. The author must specify the deterministic conversion from the spawned key to the PyTorch integer seed (or define the keys as integers).

**Classification:** Reproducibility implementation ambiguity. No choice made.

**Execution status:** Scientific implementation remains paused. The prior sixteen blockers are accepted/resolved by the author except where superseded by the five R1 blockers above. The repository-local Git identity was configured as authorized: `CFHM-CI <noreply@localhost>`.

## DEVIATION-003 — Typed-edge sampling convention remains unspecified

**Stage:** Pre-implementation audit after SPEC-001-R2.

SPEC-001 §2 states that the graph is a random DAG with three edge types and `P(edge) = {0.04, 0.08, 0.05}`, but does not specify whether, for each ordered pair `i<j`, the three type-specific Bernoulli draws are independent (allowing multiple parallel typed edges), or whether exactly one type is sampled from the three probabilities after a single edge/no-edge draw. These alternatives produce different graph densities, typed parent sets, thinning counts, features, ground-truth hazards, and model inputs.

**Classification:** Scientific graph-generator ambiguity. No convention has been selected. Scientific implementation is paused pending author binding.

## RUNTIME-001 / DEVIATION-004 — Feature dimension contradiction reached at execution

**Stage:** Single-seed smoke run, before model fitting.

**Command:**

```text
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --seeds 1000:1000 2>&1 | tee logs/smoke_seed_1000.log
```

**Exact runtime error:**

```text
Traceback (most recent call last):
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/run_experiment.py", line 753, in <module>
    main()
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/run_experiment.py", line 585, in main
    data = simulate_world(
           ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/run_experiment.py", line 314, in simulate_world
    fragility = x_std @ w_base + fragility_noise
                ~~~~~~^~~~~~~
ValueError: matmul: Input operand 1 has a mismatch in its core dimension 0, with gufunc signature (n?,k),(k,m?)->(n?,m?) (size 10 is different from 9)
```

**Root issue:** The authored feature list contains 1 log-age + 1 in-degree + 1 lognormal-popularity feature + 1 uniform-complexity feature + 5 field one-hot features = 9 columns, while SPEC-001 and R2-002/R2-010 require `d=10` and an MLP input of 10. The implementation correctly exposed the contradiction rather than adding an unrequested feature.

**Classification:** Scientific specification contradiction / implementation failure caused by underspecified feature dimension. No model fit, metric, or scientific result was produced.

**Required author binding:** Specify whether the intended dimension is 9 (and revise the MLP and w* dimension) or identify the missing tenth feature and its generation/standardization rule. No choice has been made.

## DEVIATION-005 — HANDOFF-v1.0 does not resolve RUNTIME-001 / DEVIATION-004

**Stage:** Consolidated handoff audit, before resuming execution.

HANDOFF-v1.0 repeats the feature list of four scalar features plus five field one-hot indicators, which yields 9 columns, while also repeating `Total d=10`, a 10-dimensional `w*`, and a 10-input MLP. It does not identify a tenth feature or revise the dimensionality. Because the handoff states that original specifications govern conflicts, and the originals contain the same contradiction, the runtime blocker remains open.

The handoff’s Part 1 status line also says `Scientific code executed so far: NONE`. At the time of receipt, the execution layer had already created and syntax-checked `run_experiment.py` and `score_metrics.py`, executed one smoke command for seed 1000, encountered the verbatim RUNTIME-001 traceback before model fitting, and preserved that state in commit `60dc0e3f3f9c697e2601ad311c2ebacfb35bf466`. This is recorded as a provenance/status discrepancy only; it does not change the scientific specification or the result classification.

**Classification:** Specification contradiction remains unresolved; provenance status discrepancy noted. No feature or dimension has been invented.

## DEVIATION-007 — Seed-1000 rate gate excludes both arms across all bound κ values

**Stage:** Required post-R4 smoke run, before full 50-seed execution.

**Command:**

```text
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --seeds 1000:1000 2>&1 | tee logs/smoke_seed_1000_passing.log
```

**Observed result:** The dimensional runtime error did not recur. However, seed 1000 was `RATE-EXCLUDED` in both arms because no bound κ in `{0.25, 0.5, 1, 2, 4, 0.125, 8}` produced a train-window event rate in `[0.03, 0.05]`.

| Seed | Arm | 0.125 | 0.25 | 0.5 | 1 | 2 | 4 | 8 | Status |
|---:|:---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|:---|
| 1000 | A1 | 0.879375 | 0.878221 | 0.873654 | 0.867644 | 0.852163 | 0.819952 | 0.769231 | RATE-EXCLUDED |
| 1000 | A2 | 0.646827 | 0.646394 | 0.643510 | 0.640048 | 0.627212 | 0.591683 | 0.541635 | RATE-EXCLUDED |

**Classification:** This is a data-generator/rate-gate failure, not a model-fitting failure: no learned model or metric was run. The high rates are consistent with the bound no-intercept logistic hazards and positive transmission terms under the specified graph density and ground-truth amplitude scales, but this is recorded as observed evidence rather than converted into a hypothesis conclusion.

**Scientific impact:** Seed 1000 produces no eligible F1 ranking in either arm under the pre-registered gate. The full 50-seed run is not yet launched; the author must confirm whether the intended outcome is to continue reporting potentially excluded seeds or bind an amendment to the rate-gate/data-generator specification. No post-hoc parameter change has been made.

## DEVIATION-006 — Previously undisclosed implementation decisions ratified by SPEC-001-R5

**Stage:** Post-code-review ratification, before full execution.

The following implementation decisions existed in the pre-R5 code and were not separately recorded at the time. SPEC-001-R5 now binds and ratifies them; the original recording obligation is preserved here.

| Item | Prior implementation behavior | R5 status |
|---|---|---|
| Arm-world coupling | A1 and A2 use the same base world per seed; A2 derives thinning, maintainer, and dynamics streams from integer-key offsets `+17`, `+23`, and `+31`. | Ratified by R5-001; sanctioned amendment to R-016. |
| κ semantics | κ multiplies total `a*` fragility, including `N(0,0.3)` noise, before transmission and refractory terms. | Ratified by R5-002; supersedes the earlier w*-contribution wording. |
| Target windows | Targets are weeks 2..104; fit portion is through calendar week 78 and validation is calendar weeks 79..104. | Ratified by R5-003. |
| Numerics | Hazards use logit clipping at ±60; NumPy uses integer-derived keys passed to `default_rng`; non-finite Kendall τ is recorded as 0.0. | Ratified by R5-004. |
| Bootstrap severity | Decision-grade status requires CFHM and B3 stability gates to pass on every eligible seed. | Ratified by R5-006. |
| Grid curves | Full per-epoch curves are persisted for final refits; λ-grid selection records final validation NLL per candidate. | Waived and ratified by R5-005. |
| Reserved model-init key | The spawned `model_init` key is retained in the logged key set but PyTorch uses the separately sanctioned `world_key + 4000` conversion. | Ratified as a reserved stream by R5-004/R5-005 review. |

**Scientific impact:** R5 makes these choices binding before the full run. No post-hoc result-based tuning has occurred.

## DEVIATION-008 — All 50 fixed seeds are RATE-EXCLUDED; F1 verdict arithmetic is undefined

**Stage:** Complete SPEC-001 run over seeds 1000–1049, both A1 and A2.

**Command:**

```text
OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 MKL_NUM_THREADS=1 python3 run_experiment.py --seeds 1000:1049 > logs/full_run.log 2>&1
```

**Observed raw outcome:** All 100 seed-arm cases were marked `RATE-EXCLUDED`. The raw status file contains 100 `RATE-EXCLUDED` records and 0 `ELIGIBLE` records. The required per-seed prediction CSV contains all 50 seeds, both arms, all four method labels, all 200 nodes, and blank prediction/label fields for excluded cases; it has 80,001 lines including the header.

**Scientific execution consequence:** No CFHM or baseline model was fit; therefore there are no model loss curves, λ-grid fits, bootstrap stability refits, per-seed metrics, AUROC/precision values, or stability statistics. This is a valid consequence of the bound rate-exclusion rule, not a hidden dropped-seed operation.

**Verdict consequence:** The pre-registered PASS and KILL-B3 fractions are both mathematically undefined because their denominators are zero (`0/0` over non-excluded A2 seeds). The execution layer reports `INCONCLUSIVE_RATE_EXCLUSIONS` as a non-pre-registered status label and does not convert the outcome into PASS or KILL. This label is an implementation-layer classification, not a scientific redesign or a post-hoc criterion change.

**Artifact convention:** Empty raw-table files with headers are created for final loss curves, λ-grid selection, and stability so the required artifact paths exist and explicitly represent zero eligible fits. No synthetic rows or metrics are inserted.

## DEVIATION-009 — Relay wrapper required a corrected R7 extraction boundary

**Stage:** R6/R7 artifact registration, before F1-v2 implementation.

The combined relay attachment contained two fenced amendment documents. The first extraction pass treated the closing fence of R6 as the beginning of R7 and produced a one-line blank `SPEC-001-R7-authored.md`. The exact shell output was:

```text
R6 lines=58
R7 lines=1
R6 first line=================================================================================
R7 first line=
```

The extraction boundary was corrected to the third fence block, after which R7 contained 58 lines and the expected SPEC-001-R7 header and footer. The combined relay attachment is preserved byte-for-byte at `spec/R6-R7-relay-attachment.txt`; the corrected R6 and R7 files are the binding artifacts.

**Classification:** Artifact-registration/bookkeeping error. No scientific code or F1-v1 artifact was modified.

## DEVIATION-010 — R6 minimal-diff instruction does not include the binding calibration fallback

**Stage:** Pre-R6 implementation audit.

SPEC-001-R6-002 bindingly requires a deterministic per-seed gamma bisection fallback whenever the fixed κ ladder misses the `[3%, 5%]` band. The pre-R6 implementation contains only the fixed κ ladder and the old `RATE-EXCLUDED` branch. R6’s executor instruction 2 describes a “minimal diff” of adding `GAMMA = -3.2` and inserting it into the ground-truth hazard line, with “NOTHING else changes,” but R6-002 requires additional calibration-path logic and per-seed path/final-pair recording.

**Resolution applied by the execution layer:** Apply the explicitly ordered gamma/hazard minimal diff first for the mandated smoke test. If the smoke passes, implement the R6-002 fallback and required path bookkeeping before the full run. This is an implementation necessity to satisfy the binding R6-002 requirement, not a scientific redesign; the decision and resulting diff remain separately recorded.

## DEVIATION-011 — Initial R6 smoke wrote into the F1-v1 artifact paths

**Stage:** First post-R6 smoke attempt, before F1-v2 isolation was established.

R6-003 required F1-v1 artifacts to remain immutable and new work to use a new experiment version. The initial smoke command was run from the existing repository root with the unchanged output paths in `run_experiment.py`. It therefore modified/deleted the following tracked v1 paths in the working tree: `configs/seed_1000_A1.json`, `configs/seed_1000_A2.json`, `environment.txt`, `loss_curves/full_loss_curves.csv`, `metrics/per_seed_metrics.csv`, `metrics/seed_status.json`, `metrics/stability.csv`, `metrics/verdict.json`, `metrics/verdict.md`, and `predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv`. It also created untracked `logs_v2/` and `__pycache__/`.

The baseline commit remained intact. Before restoration, the recorded hash comparison showed:

```text
metrics/seed_status.json baseline=c05a68c031bceb41af0138b0703dd2c3403a16ec22a0f21bfb796d334a6ed448
metrics/seed_status.json current=ba8ee990aacbf8b3e86c99c3423dff410744f948b660cf09d2b14e4a87879965
metrics/verdict.json baseline=d925b4415030bb8a2dcd827e7827ae5893369c30269442ce30916676067df5c0
metrics/verdict.json current=bf493a0da07aab67e4593e5722a4900f52ca83a32ecf92751a33acb07c175617
predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv baseline=9130347dc8e35420934af3aa3dd65a5b4d881fcbc58d077cef1ab79b343329ef
predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv current=dfa3b8c6713462c91b0901cb9f35a3f59ef1c2f4a23de3c9e8bf681eb35b23f6
environment.txt baseline=0f6ba327c246a86a22675fe8613fa491e2be9ce48c13a88f3766c63ef49526a6
environment.txt current=790dfe7c5b7e7a85d8918c99ccfff8e77f3334954c41e0791f6b0ab7ef6133ac
```

**Classification:** Execution/provenance isolation failure. The immutable F1-v1 commit and previously delivered archive were not changed, but the working tree was temporarily contaminated. No v1 commit will be amended. All contaminated tracked paths will be restored from `F1-v1-baseline`; the R6 smoke output will be retained only under a separate F1-v2 run root, and future v2 execution will use isolated output paths.

**Scientific impact:** No v1 scientific conclusion changes. The R6 smoke rates are evidence for the v2 smoke run only after being moved into the isolated v2 artifact root.

## DEVIATION-012 — F1-v2 smoke exposed a CFHM forecast shape bug

**Stage:** Isolated F1-v2 seed-1000 smoke, after R6 calibration and before full execution.

**Exact runtime error:**

```text
Traceback (most recent call last):
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/run_experiment.py", line 820, in <module>
    main()
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/run_experiment.py", line 713, in main
    metrics = metric_triplet(data.labels, scores)
              ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
  File "/home/ubuntu/cfhm_f1/score_metrics.py", line 36, in metric_triplet
    auroc = float(roc_auc_score(labels, scores))
                  ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
ValueError: Found input variables with inconsistent numbers of samples: [200, 1]
```

**Root cause:** `forecast_cfhm()` indexed `model.logits_from_states(... )[:, 0]`, selecting the first node across the one-step time axis and returning a one-element score vector instead of selecting the one time snapshot across all 200 nodes.

**Classification:** Implementation/measurement pipeline failure, not a scientific-design change. The v1 baseline commit remains untouched. The authorized v2 fix is to use the first time row (`[0, :]`) so CFHM produces one score per node; the R6/R7 scientific mechanism and metrics remain unchanged.

## DEVIATION-013 — R7 exclusion-array expectation conflicts with the R6-v2 outcome

**Stage:** R7 handoff construction after complete F1-v2 execution.

R7-001 S5 says both `rate_excluded_seeds` arrays must be pasted in full and gives “expected: 50 entries each.” Under the binding R6 repair, all 100 arm-seed cases were eligible through deterministic gamma bisection, so the observed A1 and A2 arrays are both empty (`[]`). The arrays will be pasted verbatim as observed; no synthetic seed IDs will be inserted to satisfy the parenthetical expectation.

**Classification:** Transmission-format expectation conflict, not a scientific result deviation. R6’s observed eligible status and complete seed ledger govern the raw evidence.

## DEVIATION-014 — Failed smoke left duplicate seed-1000 λ-grid rows in the v2 table

**Stage:** R7 artifact verification after complete F1-v2 execution.

The first isolated seed-1000 smoke reached λ-grid selection and then failed in CFHM forecast scoring (DEVIATION-012), writing three seed-1000/A1 λ-grid rows before the failure. The retry did not remove `lambda_selection.csv` because the runner’s initial cleanup removed only the prediction and full-loss files. The final table therefore contained 303 rows, with the three seed-1000/A1 rows duplicated exactly; the unique row count was 300.

The duplicate rows were:

```text
      2 1000,A1,0.0001,0.608815075377745
      2 1000,A1,0.001,0.6088150308198969
      2 1000,A1,0.01,0.6088146003342721
```

**Classification:** Artifact-contamination/retry bookkeeping error. The failed run produced no final metrics, predictions, or verdict; the duplicated λ-grid rows do not alter the full refit or derived result. The v2 λ-grid table will be deterministically deduplicated to the 300 unique rows before the handoff manifest is finalized.
```

### Module map

```markdown
# CFHM F1 Module-to-Specification Map

This map records which authored requirements each implementation or artifact file serves. It is an execution-layer provenance document and does not add scientific behavior.

| File | Authored coverage | Role in execution |
|---|---|---|
| `spec/SPEC-001-authored.txt` | Original SPEC-001 §§0–7 | Immutable original experiment specification. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R1-authored.md` | R1 resolutions for DEVIATION-001 and BLOCK-001..016 | Immutable author resolutions for the first audit. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R2-authored.md` | R2 resolutions R2-001..R2-005 | Immutable author resolutions for the second audit. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R3-authored.md` | R3-001..R3-003 | Immutable typed-edge sampling and typed-edge-indexing resolution. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R4-authored.md` | R4-001..R4-004 | Immutable nine-dimensional feature correction. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R5-authored.md` | R5-001..R5-006 | Immutable ratification of implementation decisions and artifact policy. |
| `run_experiment.py` | SPEC-001 §2–§7; R1–R5; HANDOFF C2–C7 | World generation, A1/A2 dynamics, CFHM training and forecast, baselines, bootstrap stability, raw artifact writing, and preregistered verdict computation. |
| `score_metrics.py` | SPEC-001 §5; HANDOFF C5 | Shared ranking, tie-breaking, Precision@25, Precision@10, and AUROC scoring functions. |
| `configs/seed_{seed}_{arm}.json` | SPEC-001 §7; R-016; R5-001/R5-004 | Per-run seed keys, configuration, commands, dimensions, hyperparameters, and specification hashes. |
| `data/seed_{seed}_{arm}.npz` | SPEC-001 §2; HANDOFF C2 | Raw graph arrays, features, generated events, labels, ground-truth parameters, and maintainer selections. |
| `loss_curves/full_loss_curves.csv` | SPEC-001 §3; HANDOFF C3; R5-005 | Full per-epoch loss curves for final full-window refits. |
| `loss_curves/lambda_selection.csv` | R-011; HANDOFF C3; R5-005 | Final validation NLL for each λ₁ grid candidate. |
| `predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv` | SPEC-001 §7; HANDOFF C5/C7 | Raw per-seed node predictions and labels for B1–B4, both arms, including excluded-seed status rows. |
| `metrics/per_seed_metrics.csv` | SPEC-001 §5 | Derived per-seed metrics from the shared scorer. |
| `metrics/stability.csv` | R2-004; HANDOFF C5 | Per-seed bootstrap Kendall τ values and stability gates for CFHM and B3. |
| `metrics/seed_status.json` | R-004; HANDOFF C2 | All seed/arm status and realized κ rates, including RATE-EXCLUDED seeds. |
| `metrics/verdict.json` | SPEC-001 §6; HANDOFF C6 | Raw JSON verdict computation output with per-seed arithmetic. |
| `metrics/verdict.md` | SPEC-001 §6 | Human-readable rendering of the derived verdict; not a substitute for raw tables. |
| `plots/mean_precision_at_25.png` | Diagnostic only | Programmatic diagnostic plot; raw tables remain evidence. |
| `environment.txt` | SPEC-001 §7; HANDOFF C7 | Python/platform/package and repository provenance. |
| `DEVIATIONS.md` | SPEC-001 §0; HANDOFF C7 | Complete exact-error and authorized-deviation ledger. |
| `MODULE_MAP.md` | R5 executor instruction 5 | This audit map. |

## F1-v2 and R6/R7 additions

| File | Authored coverage | Role in v2 execution |
|---|---|---|
| `spec/F1-v1-independent-audit.md` | Independent closure audit | Preserved reviewer audit that formally closes F1-v1. |
| `spec/R6-R7-relay-attachment.txt` | Relay provenance | Byte-for-byte combined R6/R7 source attachment. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R6-authored.md` | R6-001..003 | Ground-truth gamma intercept, deterministic calibration fallback, and v1 immutability. |
| `spec/SPEC-001-R7-authored.md` | R7-001..004 | Checkpoint status protocol and S1–S11 handoff format. |
| `f1_v2/STATUS.md` | R7-001 | Current checkpoint status, limited to the required fields. |
| `f1_v2/STATUS_post_smoke.md` | R7-001 | Preserved post-smoke checkpoint. |
| `f1_v2/STATUS_midpoint.md` | R7-001 | Preserved 25-seed checkpoint. |
| `f1_v2/STATUS_complete.md` | R7-001 | Preserved completion checkpoint. |
| `f1_v2/configs/*.json` | R6-002, R7 S1/S2 | Per-seed/arm calibration path, final kappa/gamma, trace, commands, and spec hashes. |
| `f1_v2/data/*.npz` | R7-002 | Raw v2 world, event, graph, feature, and calibration artifacts. |
| `f1_v2/predictions/per_seed_predictions.csv` | R7 S6/S7 | Complete v2 raw node-level predictions and labels. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/per_seed_metrics.csv` | R7 S3 | Complete 400-row v2 derived metric table. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/stability.csv` | R7 S4 | Complete 100-row v2 stability table. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/verdict.json` | R7 S5 | Run-produced v2 verdict. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/verdict_recomputed.json` | R6-003, R7 S5 | Independently recomputed v2 verdict over complete status tables. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/metrics_recomputed.json` | R7 S6 | Shared-score recomputation of all 400 metric rows. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/spot_slices.csv` | R7 S7 | Requested raw spot slices for seeds 1000/1025/1049, A1/A2, B3/B4. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/loss_summary.csv` | R7 S8 | Derived loss summary with fit counts and non-finite counts. |
| `f1_v2/metrics/artifact_manifest.sha256` | R7 S11 | SHA-256 manifest for repository artifact files. |
| `f1_v2/logs/*` | R7 S1/S10 | Full-run, checkpoint, smoke, and recomputation logs. |
| `build_handoff.py` | R7 S1–S11 | Deterministic construction of the single `HANDOFF_REPORT.md`. |
| `f1_v2/HANDOFF_REPORT.md` | R7 S1–S11 | Final evidence bundle for the author audit. |
```

## Final interpretation

F1-v2 completed all 50 seeds and 100 arm-seed cases using R6 calibration. The final run status is `INCONCLUSIVE_STABILITY_GATE`. The pre-registered KILL-B3 fraction is 0.980000, but the all-seed stability requirement is not decision-grade because the CFHM/B3 stability gates do not pass for every eligible seed. Therefore the result is not reported as KILL-B3 or PASS. It is an inconclusive stability-gate outcome, with all raw evidence preserved for independent audit.
